function detectInAppBrowser(userAgent) {
  if (!userAgent || userAgent.match(/iPhone|iPad|iPod|Android/i) === null) {
    return { isInAppBrowser: false, iabHost: undefined };
  }
  var hosts = [
    { host: 'instagram', tokens: [/Instagram/i] },
    { host: 'tiktok', tokens: [/musical_ly/i, /\btrill_/i, /BytedanceWebview/i] },
    { host: 'facebook', tokens: [/FB_IAB/i, /FBAN/i, /FBAV/i, /FBIOS/i, /\bFB4A\b/i] },
    { host: 'snapchat', tokens: [/Snapchat/i] },
    { host: 'line', tokens: [/\bLine\//i] },
    { host: 'kakaotalk', tokens: [/KAKAOTALK/i] }
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < hosts.length; i++) {
    var tokens = hosts[i].tokens;
    for (var j = 0; j < tokens.length; j++) {
      if (userAgent.match(tokens[j]) !== null) {
        return { isInAppBrowser: true, iabHost: hosts[i].host };
      }
    }
  }
  return { isInAppBrowser: false, iabHost: undefined };
}

function canSendPreloadWithoutReferrer() {
  if (!window.fetch || !window.Request) {
    return false;
  }

  try {
    return new window.Request(window.location.href, {
      referrerPolicy: 'no-referrer'
    }).referrerPolicy === 'no-referrer';
  } catch (_e) {
    return false;
  }
}
function sendPreloadLog(body) {
  var url = 'https://http-intake.logs.us5.datadoghq.com/api/v2/logs?dd-api-key=pub31a5047a3a4692afb84a423db984dc57';
  if (window.location.search || window.location.hash) {
    if (!canSendPreloadWithoutReferrer()) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      var request = window.fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Accept: 'application/json',
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        credentials: 'omit',
        referrerPolicy: 'no-referrer',
        keepalive: true,
        body: body
      });
      if (request && request['catch']) {
        request['catch'](function () {});
      }
    } catch (_e) {}
    return;
  }

  var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
  xhr.open('POST', url, true);
  xhr.setRequestHeader('Accept', 'application/json');
  xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
  xhr.send(body);
}
window.preloadErrorHandler = function (event) {
  var inAppBrowser = detectInAppBrowser(window.navigator.userAgent);

  sendPreloadLog(JSON.stringify({
    ddsource: 'browser',
    ddtags: 'env:production,version:eb13feaf',
    message: 'Browser preload error',
    preloadError: {
      errorMessage: '[REDACTED]',
      errorFile: '[REDACTED]',
      errorLine: event.lineno,
      errorColumn: event.colno,
      errorStackOrType: '[REDACTED]',
      errorJSType: typeof event.error
    },
    service: 'website',
    http: {
      useragent: window.navigator.userAgent
    },
    isInAppBrowser: inAppBrowser.isInAppBrowser,
    iabHost: inAppBrowser.iabHost,
    view: {
      url: '[REDACTED]',
      referrer: '[REDACTED]'
    }
  }));
};
// Skip reporting for the paid-social funnel cohort (marked on `/fb`) so its
// auth hops don't emit preload errors; every other visitor installs normally.
// Match the cookie name at a `; `-delimited boundary (values can't contain
// `;`) so an unrelated cookie name/value can't accidentally suppress it.
if (('; ' + document.cookie).indexOf('; rpl_iab_lp=') === -1) {
  window.addEventListener('error', window.preloadErrorHandler);
}
